We observe that under the Flow Matching framework, the standard MSE objective can be formulated as a Quadratic Form governed by a dynamically evolving Neural Tangent Kernel (NTK). This geometric perspective reveals a latent Data Interaction Matrix, where diagonal terms represent independent sample learning and off-diagonal terms encode residual correlation between heterogeneous features. Although standard training implicitly optimizes these cross-term interferences, it does so without explicit control; moreover, the prevailing data-homogeneity assumption may constrain the model's effective capacity. Motivated by this insight, we propose Semantic Granularity Alignment (SGA), using Text-to-Image synthesis as a testbed. SGA engineers targeted interventions in the vector residual field to mitigate gradient conflicts. Evaluations across DiT and U-Net architectures confirm that SGA advances the efficiency-quality trade-off by accelerating convergence and improving structural integrity.

</details>


### [76] [PolGS++: Physically-Guided Polarimetric Gaussian Splatting for Fast Reflective Surface Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10801)
*Yufei Han,Chu Zhou,Youwei Lyu,Qi Chen,Si Li,Boxin Shi,Yunpeng Jia,Heng Guo,Zhanyu Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PolGS++：一种物理引导的偏振高斯泼溅框架，用于快速反射表面重建，通过偏振BRDF模型和深度引导的可见性掩码，在10分钟内实现高质量反射表面重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反射表面的精确重建是计算机视觉中的基本挑战，在实时虚拟现实和数字内容创建中有广泛应用。虽然3D高斯泼溅（3DGS）能够实现高效的新视角渲染，但在反射表面上的性能仍落后于隐式神经方法，特别是在恢复精细几何和表面法线方面。

Method: 提出PolGS++框架：1）将偏振BRDF模型集成到3DGS中，显式解耦漫反射和镜面反射分量；2）引入深度引导的可见性掩码获取机制，实现基于偏振角的正切空间一致性约束，无需昂贵的射线追踪交叉计算。

Result: 在合成和真实世界数据集上的广泛实验验证了该方法的有效性。该物理引导设计提高了重建质量和效率，仅需约10分钟的训练时间。

Conclusion: PolGS++通过物理引导的偏振高斯泼溅框架，成功解决了反射表面重建的挑战，在质量和效率上都取得了显著提升，为实时虚拟现实和数字内容创建应用提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Accurate reconstruction of reflective surfaces remains a fundamental challenge in computer vision, with broad applications in real-time virtual reality and digital content creation. Although 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) enables efficient novel-view rendering with explicit representations, its performance on reflective surfaces still lags behind implicit neural methods, especially in recovering fine geometry and surface normals. To address this gap, we propose PolGS++, a physically-guided polarimetric Gaussian Splatting framework for fast reflective surface reconstruction. Specifically, we integrate a polarized BRDF (pBRDF) model into 3DGS to explicitly decouple diffuse and specular components, providing physically grounded reflectance modeling and stronger geometric cues for reflective surface recovery. Furthermore, we introduce a depth-guided visibility mask acquisition mechanism that enables angle-of-polarization (AoP)-based tangent-space consistency constraints in Gaussian Splatting without costly ray-tracing intersections. This physically guided design improves reconstruction quality and efficiency, requiring only about 10 minutes of training. Extensive experiments on both synthetic and real-world datasets validate the effectiveness of our method.

</details>


### [77] [Backdoor Directions in Vision Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10806)
*Sengim Karayalcin,Marina Krcek,Pin-Yu Chen,Stjepan Picek*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文研究了视觉Transformer中后门攻击的内部表征机制，发现了与触发器对应的线性方向，并利用该方向作为诊断工具分析后门特征的处理过程，提出了基于权重的检测方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机是理解后门攻击在视觉Transformer中的内部表征机制，通过机制可解释性方法来诊断和解决计算机视觉中的安全漏洞问题。

Method: 假设已知触发器，识别模型激活中的"触发器方向"，通过激活空间和参数空间的干预实验验证其因果作用，将该方向作为诊断工具追踪后门特征在层间的处理过程，并基于权重提出数据无关的检测方案。

Result: 研究发现静态补丁触发器和分布式隐蔽触发器遵循不同的内部逻辑，PGD对抗扰动可以激活/去激活已识别的触发机制，机制可解释性为诊断和解决安全漏洞提供了稳健框架。

Conclusion: 机制可解释性为诊断和解决计算机视觉模型中的安全漏洞提供了有效的分析框架，能够揭示不同类型的后门攻击的内部工作机制差异。

Abstract: This paper investigates how Backdoor Attacks are represented within Vision Transformers (ViTs). By assuming knowledge of the trigger, we identify a specific ``trigger direction'' in the model's activations that corresponds to the internal representation of the trigger. We confirm the causal role of this linear direction by showing that interventions in both activation and parameter space consistently modulate the model's backdoor behavior across multiple datasets and attack types. Using this direction as a diagnostic tool, we trace how backdoor features are processed across layers. Our analysis reveals distinct qualitative differences: static-patch triggers follow a different internal logic than stealthy, distributed triggers. We further examine the link between backdoors and adversarial attacks, specifically testing whether PGD-based perturbations (de-)activate the identified trigger mechanism. Finally, we propose a data-free, weight-based detection scheme for stealthy-trigger attacks. Our findings show that mechanistic interpretability offers a robust framework for diagnosing and addressing security vulnerabilities in computer vision.

</details>


### [78] [HanMoVLM: Large Vision-Language Models for Professional Artistic Painting Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10814)
*Hongji Yang,Yucheng Zhou,Wencheng Han,Songlian Li,Xiaotong Zhao,Jianbing Shen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出HanMoVLM，将大视觉语言模型转化为中国艺术领域的专业绘画评估专家，通过专家验证的思维链和奖励函数提升评估准确性，并用于提升中国画生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的大视觉语言模型在通用视觉任务上表现优秀，但在艺术领域缺乏专业性，无法像人类专家一样对特定艺术领域的作品进行专业评估，特别是在中国艺术领域这种更抽象、需要专业训练才能评估的领域。

Method: 1. 构建HanMo-Bench数据集，包含真实的拍卖级大师作品和AI生成作品，基于真实市场估值；2. 提出HanMoVLM模型，构建专家验证的思维链，引导模型进行专家级推理：从内容识别、兴趣区域定位到专业评估；3. 设计奖励函数来优化HanMoVLM的推理过程；4. 将HanMoVLM作为测试时扩展的关键骨干，用于图像生成中的高质量验证。

Result: 实验和人类研究证实，HanMoVLM有效弥补了现有模型的不足，与专业专家评估高度一致，显著提升了中国画生成的质量。

Conclusion: 该研究成功将大视觉语言模型转化为中国艺术领域的专业评估专家，通过专家验证的思维链和优化方法，实现了与人类专家相当的专业评估能力，并能有效提升艺术作品的生成质量。

Abstract: While Large Vision-Language Models (VLMs) demonstrate impressive general visual capabilities, they remain artistically blind and unable to offer professional evaluation of artworks within specific artistic domains like human experts. To bridge this gap, we transform VLMs into experts capable of professional-grade painting evaluation in the Chinese Artistic Domain, which is more abstract and demands extensive artistic training for evaluation. We introduce HanMo-Bench, a new dataset that features authentic auction-grade masterpieces and AI-generated works, grounded in real-world market valuations. To realize the rigorous judgment, we propose the HanMoVLM and construct a Chain-of-Thought (CoT) validated by experts. This CoT guides the model to perform expert-level reasoning: from content identification and Region of Interest (RoI) localization to professional evaluation, guided by both theme-specific evaluation and typical three-tier evaluation in Chinese paintings. Furthermore, we design a reward function to refine the reasoning process of the HanMoVLM to improve the accuracy. We demonstrate that HanMoVLM can serve as a critical backbone for Test-time Scaling in image generation. By acting as a high-quality verifier, HanMoVLM enables generative models to select the most artistically superior outputs from multiple candidates. Experimental results and human studies confirm that the proposed HanMoVLM effectively bridges the gap, achieving a high consistency with professional experts and significantly improving the quality of Chinese Painting generation.

</details>


### [79] [A dataset of medication images with instance segmentation masks for preventing adverse drug events](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10825)
*W. I. Chu,S. Hirani,G. Tarroni,L. Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MEDISEG是一个包含32种药丸类型、8262张图像的实例分割数据集，专门针对真实世界复杂场景（如药丸重叠、遮挡、光照变化），用于训练和评估AI药丸识别模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 药物错误和不良药物事件对患者安全构成重大风险，现有AI药丸识别模型缺乏能够捕捉真实世界复杂性的综合数据集，如药丸重叠、光照变化和遮挡等场景。

Method: 创建MEDISEG数据集，包含32种不同药丸类型的实例分割标注，涵盖从单个药丸图像到杂乱药盒的各种条件。使用该数据集训练YOLOv8和YOLOv9模型，并评估其在少量样本检测协议下的表现。

Result: 在MEDISEG上训练的模型在3-Pills子集上达到99.5%的mAP@0.5，在32-Pills子集上达到80.1%。与现有数据集相比，基于MEDISEG的基础训练显著提高了在遮挡多药丸场景中对未见药丸类别的识别能力。

Conclusion: MEDISEG不仅支持鲁棒的监督训练，还能在有限监督下促进可迁移表示的学习，是开发和评估用于药物安全的AI驱动系统的宝贵资源。

Abstract: Medication errors and adverse drug events (ADEs) pose significant risks to patient safety, often arising from difficulties in reliably identifying pharmaceuticals in real-world settings. AI-based pill recognition models offer a promising solution, but the lack of comprehensive datasets hinders their development. Existing pill image datasets rarely capture real-world complexities such as overlapping pills, varied lighting, and occlusions. MEDISEG addresses this gap by providing instance segmentation annotations for 32 distinct pill types across 8262 images, encompassing diverse conditions from individual pill images to cluttered dosette boxes. We trained YOLOv8 and YOLOv9 on MEDISEG to demonstrate their usability, achieving mean average precision at IoU 0.5 of 99.5 percent on the 3-Pills subset and 80.1 percent on the 32-Pills subset. We further evaluate MEDISEG under a few-shot detection protocol, demonstrating that base training on MEDISEG significantly improves recognition of unseen pill classes in occluded multi-pill scenarios compared to existing datasets. These results highlight the dataset's ability not only to support robust supervised training but also to promote transferable representations under limited supervision, making it a valuable resource for developing and benchmarking AI-driven systems for medication safety.

</details>


### [80] [Evaluating Few-Shot Pill Recognition Under Visual Domain Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10833)
*W. I. Chu,G. Tarroni,L. Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究从部署角度探讨少样本药片识别，关注真实跨数据集域偏移下的泛化能力，而非架构创新。研究发现语义分类在少量样本下快速适应，但重叠遮挡条件下定位和召回显著下降，强调训练数据真实性和少样本微调对部署准备的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 药物不良事件是重要的可预防伤害来源，自动药片识别系统可提升用药安全。但现实部署面临视觉复杂条件（杂乱场景、重叠药片、反光、多样化采集环境）的阻碍，需要研究从部署角度出发的少样本药片识别方法。

Method: 采用两阶段目标检测框架：基础训练后接少样本微调。使用每类1、5或10个标记样本适应新药片类别，在包含多目标杂乱场景的独立部署数据集上评估。使用分类中心和基于错误的指标来应对异构标注策略。

Result: 语义药片识别在少样本监督下快速适应，分类性能即使只有一个标记样本也能达到饱和。但在重叠和遮挡条件下的压力测试显示，尽管语义分类稳健，定位和召回显著下降。在视觉真实的多药片数据上训练的模型在少样本场景中表现出更强的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 少样本药片识别在语义分类方面表现良好，但在现实复杂条件下的定位能力有限。训练数据的真实性和少样本微调对部署准备至关重要，可作为诊断工具评估模型在真实世界条件下的性能。

Abstract: Adverse drug events are a significant source of preventable harm, which has led to the development of automated pill recognition systems to enhance medication safety. Real-world deployment of these systems is hindered by visually complex conditions, including cluttered scenes, overlapping pills, reflections, and diverse acquisition environments. This study investigates few-shot pill recognition from a deployment-oriented perspective, prioritizing generalization under realistic cross-dataset domain shifts over architectural innovation. A two-stage object detection framework is employed, involving base training followed by few-shot fine-tuning. Models are adapted to novel pill classes using one, five, or ten labeled examples per class and are evaluated on a separate deployment dataset featuring multi-object, cluttered scenes. The evaluation focuses on classification-centric and error-based metrics to address heterogeneous annotation strategies. Findings indicate that semantic pill recognition adapts rapidly with few-shot supervision, with classification performance reaching saturation even with a single labeled example. However, stress testing under overlapping and occluded conditions demonstrates a marked decline in localization and recall, despite robust semantic classification. Models trained on visually realistic, multi-pill data consistently exhibit greater robustness in low-shot scenarios, underscoring the importance of training data realism and the diagnostic utility of few-shot fine-tuning for deployment readiness.

</details>


### [81] [UltrasoundAgents: Hierarchical Multi-Agent Evidence-Chain Reasoning for Breast Ultrasound Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10852)
*Yali Zhu,Kang Zhou,Dingbang Wu,Gaofeng Meng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出UltrasoundAgents分层多智能体框架，通过主智能体定位病灶、子智能体分析局部特征、主智能体整合证据的临床对齐流程，结合解耦渐进训练策略提升乳腺癌超声诊断的可解释性和准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有乳腺超声诊断方法多为端到端预测或仅提供弱证据支持，难以捕捉细粒度病灶线索，且缺乏可审计性和临床可审查性。需要与临床工作流程对齐并提高证据可追溯性。

Method: 提出分层多智能体框架UltrasoundAgents：主智能体在全图中定位病灶并触发裁剪放大操作；子智能体分析局部视图预测四个临床相关属性（回声模式、钙化、边界类型、边缘形态）；主智能体整合这些结构化属性进行基于证据的推理，输出BI-RADS分类和恶性预测。采用解耦渐进训练策略：先训练属性智能体，再用真实属性训练主智能体学习属性推理，最后通过轨迹自蒸馏和空间监督进行监督微调。

Result: 实验表明，相比强视觉语言基线，该方法在诊断准确性和属性一致性方面取得一致提升，同时提供结构化证据和可追溯的推理过程。

Conclusion: UltrasoundAgents框架通过分层多智能体设计与临床工作流程对齐，结合渐进训练策略，实现了更准确、可解释的乳腺超声诊断，为临床审查提供了结构化证据支持。

Abstract: Breast ultrasound diagnosis typically proceeds from global lesion localization to local sign assessment and then evidence integration to assign a BI-RADS category and determine benignity or malignancy. Many existing methods rely on end-to-end prediction or provide only weakly grounded evidence, which can miss fine-grained lesion cues and limit auditability and clinical review. To align with the clinical workflow and improve evidence traceability, we propose a hierarchical multi-agent framework, termed UltrasoundAgents. A main agent localizes the lesion in the full image and triggers a crop-and-zoom operation. A sub-agent analyzes the local view and predicts four clinically relevant attributes, namely echogenicity pattern, calcification, boundary type, and edge (margin) morphology. The main agent then integrates these structured attributes to perform evidence-based reasoning and output the BI-RADS category and the malignancy prediction, while producing reviewable intermediate evidence. Furthermore, hierarchical multi-agent training often suffers from error propagation, difficult credit assignment, and sparse rewards. To alleviate this and improve training stability, we introduce a decoupled progressive training strategy. We first train the attribute agent, then train the main agent with oracle attributes to learn robust attribute-based reasoning, and finally apply corrective trajectory self-distillation with spatial supervision to build high-quality trajectories for supervised fine-tuning, yielding a deployable end-to-end policy. Experiments show consistent gains over strong vision-language baselines in diagnostic accuracy and attribute agreement, together with structured evidence and traceable reasoning.

</details>


### [82] [Beyond Sequential Distance: Inter-Modal Distance Invariant Position Encoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10863)
*Lin Chen,Bolin Ni,Qi Yang,Zili Wang,Kun Ding,Ying Wang,Houwen Peng,Shiming Xiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出DIPE位置编码机制，解决MLLMs在长上下文场景中的视觉衰减问题，通过解耦模态间位置编码保持视觉感知一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型在长上下文场景中存在视觉衰减问题，即随着文本序列增长，对视觉token的关注度下降，导致文本生成脱离视觉约束。作者认为这源于多模态RoPE的内在归纳偏置，随着视觉和文本token距离增加而惩罚模态间注意力。

Method: 提出跨模态距离不变位置编码(DIPE)，基于模态交互解耦位置编码。保留模态内交互的自然相对位置以维持局部结构，同时对模态间交互实施锚定的感知邻近性。该策略有效减轻基于模态间距离的惩罚，确保视觉信号在不同上下文长度下保持感知一致性。

Result: 实验结果表明，将DIPE与多模态RoPE结合后，模型在长上下文场景中保持稳定的视觉基础，显著缓解视觉衰减，同时在标准短上下文基准测试中保持性能。

Conclusion: DIPE是一种简单有效的机制，通过解耦模态间位置编码解决了MLLMs在长上下文中的视觉衰减问题，使视觉信号在不同上下文长度下保持感知一致性，提升了模型的视觉基础能力。

Abstract: Despite the remarkable capabilities of Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs), they still suffer from visual fading in long-context scenarios. Specifically, the attention to visual tokens diminishes as the text sequence lengthens, leading to text generation detached from visual constraints. We attribute this degradation to the inherent inductive bias of Multimodal RoPE, which penalizes inter-modal attention as the distance between visual and text tokens increases. To address this, we propose inter-modal Distance Invariant Position Encoding (DIPE), a simple but effective mechanism that disentangles position encoding based on modality interactions. DIPE retains the natural relative positioning for intra-modal interactions to preserve local structure, while enforcing an anchored perceptual proximity for inter-modal interactions. This strategy effectively mitigates the inter-modal distance-based penalty, ensuring that visual signals remain perceptually consistent regardless of the context length. Experimental results demonstrate that by integrating DIPE with Multimodal RoPE, the model maintains stable visual grounding in long-context scenarios, significantly alleviating visual fading while preserving performance on standard short-context benchmarks. Code is available at https://github.com/lchen1019/DIPE.

</details>


### [83] [Bilevel Layer-Positioning LoRA for Real Image Dehazing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10872)
*Yan Zhang,Long Ma,Yuxin Feng,Zhe Huang,Fan Zhou,Zhuo Su*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出BiLaLoRA方法，利用CLIP跨模态能力实现无监督去雾，通过双层定位LoRA策略自适应调整关键网络层


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于学习的真实图像去雾方法在多样真实雾霾场景中面临适应挑战，主要问题包括缺乏有效的无监督机制和全模型微调成本高昂

Method: 1) 提出雾到清晰文本导向损失，利用CLIP跨模态能力将去雾重构为潜在空间语义对齐问题；2) 引入双层定位LoRA策略，同时学习LoRA参数并自动搜索注入层，实现关键网络层的针对性适应

Result: 在多个真实世界去雾基准测试中表现出优于现有最先进方法的性能

Conclusion: 该方法通过跨模态语义对齐和自适应网络层调整，有效解决了真实图像去雾中的适应挑战，代码已开源

Abstract: Learning-based real image dehazing methods have achieved notable progress, yet they still face adaptation challenges in diverse real haze scenes. These challenges mainly stem from the lack of effective unsupervised mechanisms for unlabeled data and the heavy cost of full model fine-tuning. To address these challenges, we propose the haze-to-clear text-directed loss that leverages CLIP's cross-modal capabilities to reformulate real image dehazing as a semantic alignment problem in latent space, thereby providing explicit unsupervised cross-modal guidance in the absence of reference images. Furthermore, we introduce the Bilevel Layer-positioning LoRA (BiLaLoRA) strategy, which learns both the LoRA parameters and automatically search the injection layers, enabling targeted adaptation of critical network layers. Extensive experiments demonstrate our superiority against state-of-the-art methods on multiple real-world dehazing benchmarks. The code is publicly available at https://github.com/YanZhang-zy/BiLaLoRA.

</details>


### [84] [S2D: Sparse to Dense Lifting for 3D Reconstruction with Minimal Inputs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10893)
*Yuzhou Ji,Qijian Tian,He Zhu,Xiaoqi Jiang,Guangzhi Cao,Lizhuang Ma,Yuan Xie,Xin Tan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: S2D：通过两步提升策略，从稀疏输入实现高质量3D高斯泼溅重建的新方法


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D表示方法（点云和3D高斯泼溅）在稀疏输入下存在渲染不真实和性能显著下降的问题，需要一种能从最小化输入实现高质量3D重建的解决方案。

Method: 提出S2D（稀疏到密集提升）管道：1）高效一步扩散模型提升稀疏点云质量，修复图像伪影；2）随机样本丢弃和加权梯度的重建策略，从稀疏输入视图到密集新视图实现鲁棒模型拟合。

Result: S2D在不同输入稀疏度下实现了最佳的新视图生成一致性和第一梯队的稀疏视图重建质量，在现有方法中使用最少的捕获次数重建稳定场景。

Conclusion: S2D通过桥接点云和3D高斯泼溅表示，实现了最小化输入需求的高质量3DGS重建，为3DGS应用提供了实用的解决方案。

Abstract: Explicit 3D representations have already become an essential medium for 3D simulation and understanding. However, the most commonly used point cloud and 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) each suffer from non-photorealistic rendering and significant degradation under sparse inputs. In this paper, we introduce Sparse to Dense lifting (S2D), a novel pipeline that bridges the two representations and achieves high-quality 3DGS reconstruction with minimal inputs. Specifically, the S2D lifting is two-fold. We first present an efficient one-step diffusion model that lifts sparse point cloud for high-fidelity image artifact fixing. Meanwhile, to reconstruct 3D consistent scenes, we also design a corresponding reconstruction strategy with random sample drop and weighted gradient for robust model fitting from sparse input views to dense novel views. Extensive experiments show that S2D achieves the best consistency in generating novel view guidance and first-tier sparse view reconstruction quality under different input sparsity. By reconstructing stable scenes with the least possible captures among existing methods, S2D enables minimal input requirements for 3DGS applications.

</details>


### [85] [Novel Architecture of RPA In Oral Cancer Lesion Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10928)
*Revana Magdy,Joy Naoum,Ali Hamdi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: OC-RPAv2通过单例模式和批处理技术，将口腔癌病变检测效率提升60-100倍，预测时间从0.29秒/图像降至0.06秒/图像


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 口腔癌病变的准确早期检测对有效诊断和治疗至关重要，需要提高检测系统的效率和可扩展性以降低医疗成本

Method: 开发了两个RPA实现：OC-RPAv1（单图像处理）和OC-RPAv2（采用单例设计模式和批处理技术），在31张图像测试集上进行评估

Result: OC-RPAv1平均预测时间0.29秒/图像，OC-RPAv2降至0.06秒/图像，效率提升60-100倍，显著减少了计算时间和成本

Conclusion: 设计模式和批处理技术能够显著提升口腔癌检测系统的可扩展性和效率，为医疗应用提供了更经济高效的解决方案

Abstract: Accurate and early detection of oral cancer lesions is crucial for effective diagnosis and treatment. This study evaluates two RPA implementations, OC-RPAv1 and OC-RPAv2, using a test set of 31 images. OC-RPAv1 processes one image per prediction in an average of 0.29 seconds, while OCRPAv2 employs a Singleton design pattern and batch processing, reducing prediction time to just 0.06 seconds per image. This represents a 60-100x efficiency improvement over standard RPA methods, showcasing that design patterns and batch processing can enhance scalability and reduce costs in oral cancer detection

</details>


### [86] [Bridging the Skill Gap in Clinical CBCT Interpretation with CBCTRepD](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10933)
*Qinxin Wu,Fucheng Niu,Hengchuan Zhu,Yifan Sun,Ye Shen,Xu Li,Han Wu,Leqi Liu,Zhiwen Pan,Zuozhu Liu,Fudong Zhu,Bin Feng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CBCTRepD是一个用于口腔颌面CBCT报告生成的双语AI系统，通过大规模高质量数据集和临床评估框架，在放射科医生-AI协作中显著提升报告质量


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI在医学报告生成方面进展迅速，但在口腔颌面CBCT报告中的应用有限，主要原因是高质量配对的CBCT-报告数据稀缺，以及CBCT体积图像解释的固有复杂性

Method: 1. 构建大规模高质量配对CBCT-报告数据集（7,408个研究，覆盖55种口腔疾病）；2. 开发CBCTRepD双语报告生成系统；3. 建立临床多级评估框架，评估AI生成草稿和放射科医生编辑的合作报告

Result: CBCTRepD在报告生成性能上表现优异，写作质量和标准化程度与中级放射科医生相当。在放射科医生-AI协作中，帮助初级放射科医生提升至中级水平，中级放射科医生接近高级水平，高级放射科医生减少遗漏错误

Conclusion: CBCTRepD通过改善报告结构、减少遗漏、促进对跨解剖区域共存病变的关注，显示出作为实际CBCT报告助手的强大潜力，适用于多级医疗环境

Abstract: Generative AI has advanced rapidly in medical report generation; however, its application to oral and maxillofacial CBCT reporting remains limited, largely because of the scarcity of high-quality paired CBCT-report data and the intrinsic complexity of volumetric CBCT interpretation. To address this, we introduce CBCTRepD, a bilingual oral and maxillofacial CBCT report-generation system designed for integration into routine radiologist-AI co-authoring workflows. We curated a large-scale, high-quality paired CBCT-report dataset comprising approximately 7,408 studies, covering 55 oral disease entities across diverse acquisition settings, and used it to develop the system. We further established a clinically grounded, multi-level evaluation framework that assesses both direct AI-generated drafts and radiologist-edited collaboration reports using automatic metrics together with radiologist- and clinician-centered evaluation. Using this framework, we show that CBCTRepD achieves superior report-generation performance and produces drafts with writing quality and standardization comparable to those of intermediate radiologists. More importantly, in radiologist-AI collaboration, CBCTRepD provides consistent and clinically meaningful benefits across experience levels: it helps novice radiologists improve toward intermediate-level reporting, enables intermediate radiologists to approach senior-level performance, and even assists senior radiologists by reducing omission-related errors, including clinically important missed lesions. By improving report structure, reducing omissions, and promoting attention to co-existing lesions across anatomical regions, CBCTRepD shows strong and reliable potential as a practical assistant for real-world CBCT reporting across multi-level care settings.

</details>


### [87] [Pointy - A Lightweight Transformer for Point Cloud Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10963)
*Konrad Szafer,Marek Kraft,Dominik Belter*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 轻量级Transformer点云架构仅用39k点云训练，性能超越使用200k+样本的大型基础模型，接近使用百万级多模态数据的SOTA结果


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前点云基础模型通常依赖大规模跨模态监督学习，本文旨在探索更轻量、更可控的方法，通过精心设计的架构和训练设置实现高效的点云表示学习

Method: 提出轻量级Transformer点云架构，仅使用39k点云数据进行训练（无跨模态监督），并进行全面的复制研究，标准化训练方案和基准测试，以隔离架构选择的影响

Result: 模型性能超越多个使用200k+训练样本的大型基础模型，接近使用百万级点云、图像和文本样本的SOTA结果，证明了简单骨干网络在精心设计下也能取得竞争力

Conclusion: 精心设计的训练设置和架构比单纯增加数据规模或模型复杂度更重要，简单的无tokenizer架构能够提供与复杂或数据丰富策略相竞争的结果

Abstract: Foundation models for point cloud data have recently grown in capability, often leveraging extensive representation learning from language or vision. In this work, we take a more controlled approach by introducing a lightweight transformer-based point cloud architecture. In contrast to the heavy reliance on cross-modal supervision, our model is trained only on 39k point clouds - yet it outperforms several larger foundation models trained on over 200k training samples. Interestingly, our method approaches state-of-the-art results from models that have seen over a million point clouds, images, and text samples, demonstrating the value of a carefully curated training setup and architecture. To ensure rigorous evaluation, we conduct a comprehensive replication study that standardizes the training regime and benchmarks across multiple point cloud architectures. This unified experimental framework isolates the impact of architectural choices, allowing for transparent comparisons and highlighting the benefits of our design and other tokenizer-free architectures. Our results show that simple backbones can deliver competitive results to more complex or data-rich strategies. The implementation, including code, pre-trained models, and training protocols, is available at https://github.com/KonradSzafer/Pointy.

</details>


### [88] [Contrastive learning-based video quality assessment-jointed video vision transformer for video recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10965)
*Jian Sun,Mohammad H. Mahoor*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SSL-V3方法，结合自监督学习的视频视觉Transformer和无参考视频质量评估，通过联合学习机制将视频质量评估融入视频分类，解决视频质量对分类性能的影响问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究发现视频质量显著影响视频分类性能，特别是在轻度认知障碍分类任务中，清晰视频分类效果好而模糊视频效果差。意识到将视频质量评估(VQA)引入视频分类可能提升性能，但视频数据集通常缺乏VQA标签，无法提供准确的视频质量分数。

Method: 提出SSL-V3方法：基于自监督学习的视频视觉Transformer结合无参考VQA。采用Combined-SSL机制，将视频质量分数作为因子直接调整视频分类的特征图，同时以该分数为交叉点连接VQA和分类任务，利用有监督的分类任务来调整VQA参数。

Result: 在两个数据集上获得稳健的实验结果，在I-CONECT医疗数据集（包含面部视频访谈）上达到94.87%的准确率，验证了SSL-V3的有效性。

Conclusion: SSL-V3通过联合学习机制成功将视频质量评估融入视频分类，解决了视频质量对分类性能的影响问题，特别是在医疗视频分析领域表现出色。

Abstract: Video quality significantly affects video classification. We found this problem when we classified Mild Cognitive Impairment well from clear videos, but worse from blurred ones. From then, we realized that referring to Video Quality Assessment (VQA) may improve video classification. This paper proposed Self-Supervised Learning-based Video Vision Transformer combined with No-reference VQA for video classification (SSL-V3) to fulfill the goal. SSL-V3 leverages Combined-SSL mechanism to join VQA into video classification and address the label shortage of VQA, which commonly occurs in video datasets, making it impossible to provide an accurate Video Quality Score. In brief, Combined-SSL takes video quality score as a factor to directly tune the feature map of the video classification. Then, the score, as an intersected point, links VQA and classification, using the supervised classification task to tune the parameters of VQA. SSL-V3 achieved robust experimental results on two datasets. For example, it reached an accuracy of 94.87% on some interview videos in the I-CONECT (a facial video-involved healthcare dataset), verifying SSL-V3's effectiveness.

</details>


### [89] [Med-DualLoRA: Local Adaptation of Foundation Models for 3D Cardiac MRI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10967)
*Joan Perramon-Llussà,Amelia Jiménez-Sánchez,Grzegorz Skorupko,Fotis Avgoustidis,Carlos Martín-Isla,Karim Lekadir,Polyxeni Gkontra*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Med-DualLoRA框架，通过解耦全局和本地LoRA模块，在保护隐私的同时实现医疗基础模型的高效联邦微调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医疗基础模型在医学影像任务中表现优异，但单中心微调可能导致性能不佳和模型偏差，而集中式微调又受隐私限制。传统联邦学习方法在异构多中心数据下效果有限且通信开销大。

Method: 提出Med-DualLoRA框架，采用参数高效微调(PEFT)方法，通过加性分解解耦全局共享和本地低秩适配(LoRA)模块。仅共享和聚合全局组件，本地适配器保持私有，且仅微调两个transformer块以提升效率。

Result: 在ACDC和M&M数据集上的3D CMR疾病检测任务中，Med-DualLoRA达到平衡准确率0.768和特异性0.612，显著优于其他联邦PEFT基线方法，同时保持通信效率。

Conclusion: Med-DualLoRA为医疗基础模型在真实临床约束下的本地联邦适应提供了可扩展解决方案，在保护隐私的同时实现了性能提升和通信效率优化。

Abstract: Foundation models (FMs) show great promise for robust downstream performance across medical imaging tasks and modalities, including cardiac magnetic resonance (CMR), following task-specific adaptation. However, adaptation using single-site data may lead to suboptimal performance and increased model bias, while centralized fine-tuning on clinical data is often infeasible due to privacy constraints. Federated fine-tuning offers a privacy-preserving alternative; yet conventional approaches struggle under heterogeneous, non-IID multi-center data and incur substantial communication overhead when adapting large models. In this work, we study federated FM fine-tuning for 3D CMR disease detection and propose Med-DualLoRA, a client-aware parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) federated framework that disentangles globally shared and local low-rank adaptations (LoRA) through additive decomposition. Global and local LoRA modules are trained locally, but only the global component is shared and aggregated across sites, keeping local adapters private. This design improves personalization while significantly reducing communication cost, and experiments show that adapting only two transformer blocks preserves performance while further improving efficiency. We evaluate our method on a multi-center state-of-the-art cine 3D CMR FM fine-tuned for disease detection using ACDC and combined M\&Ms datasets, treating each vendor as a federated client. Med-DualLoRA achieves statistically significant improved performance (balanced accuracy 0.768, specificity 0.612) compared to other federated PEFT baselines, while maintaining communication efficiency. Our approach provides a scalable solution for local federated adaptation of medical FMs under realistic clinical constraints.

</details>


### [90] [VCR: Variance-Driven Channel Recalibration for Robust Low-Light Enhancement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10975)
*Zhixin Cheng,Fangwen Zhang,Xiaotian Yin,Baoqun Yin,Haodian Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出VCR框架，通过方差驱动通道重校准解决低光图像增强中亮度与色度通道不一致及颜色分布错位问题


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法存在亮度与色度通道不一致问题，导致颜色分布错位和不自然的增强结果。sRGB空间方法存在亮度与颜色纠缠问题，HSV空间解耦不足且引入噪声，HVI空间虽改进但仍有通道不一致问题。

Method: 提出VCR框架，包含两个核心模块：1) 通道自适应调整(CAA)模块，使用方差引导特征过滤增强对高强度和颜色分布区域的关注；2) 颜色分布对齐(CDA)模块，在颜色特征空间强制执行分布对齐。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上的实验结果表明，该方法相比现有方法实现了最先进的性能，提升了低光条件下的感知质量。

Conclusion: VCR框架通过方差驱动通道重校准有效解决了低光图像增强中的通道不一致和颜色分布错位问题，实现了更自然的增强效果。

Abstract: Most sRGB-based LLIE methods suffer from entangled luminance and color, while the HSV color space offers insufficient decoupling at the cost of introducing significant red and black noise artifacts. Recently, the HVI color space has been proposed to address these limitations by enhancing color fidelity through chrominance polarization and intensity compression. However, existing methods could suffer from channel-level inconsistency between luminance and chrominance, and misaligned color distribution may lead to unnatural enhancement results. To address these challenges, we propose the Variance-Driven Channel Recalibration for Robust Low-Light Enhancement (VCR), a novel framework for low-light image enhancement. VCR consists of two main components, including the Channel Adaptive Adjustment (CAA) module, which employs variance-guided feature filtering to enhance the model's focus on regions with high intensity and color distribution. And the Color Distribution Alignment (CDA) module, which enforces distribution alignment in the color feature space. These designs enhance perceptual quality under low-light conditions. Experimental results on several benchmark datasets demonstrate that the proposed method achieves state-of-the-art performance compared with existing methods.

</details>


### [91] [Too Vivid to Be Real? Benchmarking and Calibrating Generative Color Fidelity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10990)
*Zhengyao Fang,Zexi Jia,Yijia Zhong,Pengcheng Luo,Jinchao Zhang,Guangming Lu,Jun Yu,Wenjie Pei*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Color Fidelity Dataset (CFD)和Color Fidelity Metric (CFM)来评估和改进文本到图像生成的颜色保真度，解决现有评估偏向鲜艳夸张图像的问题，并提出了无需训练的颜色保真度优化方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本到图像生成系统虽然视觉质量有所提升，但生成的图像往往过于鲜艳夸张，缺乏真实照片的视觉真实性。这主要是由于现有评估范式存在偏差：人类评分和偏好训练的指标倾向于饱和度和对比度夸张的图像，导致即使提示生成写实风格图像，结果也过于鲜艳而不真实。

Method: 1) 构建包含130万张真实和合成图像的颜色保真度数据集(CFD)，包含不同颜色真实度级别的有序标注；2) 开发基于多模态编码器的颜色保真度指标(CFM)来学习感知颜色保真度；3) 提出无需训练的颜色保真度优化(CFR)方法，通过自适应调节生成过程中的时空引导尺度来增强颜色真实性。

Result: CFD数据集支持CFM进行评估，CFM学习到的注意力进一步指导CFR优化文本到图像生成的颜色保真度，形成了一个渐进式评估和改进写实风格文本到图像生成颜色保真度的完整框架。数据集和代码已开源。

Conclusion: 该工作通过构建数据集、评估指标和优化方法的三位一体框架，系统性地解决了文本到图像生成中颜色保真度不足的问题，为生成更真实照片风格的图像提供了有效的评估和改进工具。

Abstract: Recent advances in text-to-image (T2I) generation have greatly improved visual quality, yet producing images that appear visually authentic to real-world photography remains challenging. This is partly due to biases in existing evaluation paradigms: human ratings and preference-trained metrics often favor visually vivid images with exaggerated saturation and contrast, which make generations often too vivid to be real even when prompted for realistic-style images. To address this issue, we present Color Fidelity Dataset (CFD) and Color Fidelity Metric (CFM) for objective evaluation of color fidelity in realistic-style generations. CFD contains over 1.3M real and synthetic images with ordered levels of color realism, while CFM employs a multimodal encoder to learn perceptual color fidelity. In addition, we propose a training-free Color Fidelity Refinement (CFR) that adaptively modulates spatial-temporal guidance scale in generation, thereby enhancing color authenticity. Together, CFD supports CFM for assessment, whose learned attention further guides CFR to refine T2I fidelity, forming a progressive framework for assessing and improving color fidelity in realistic-style T2I generation. The dataset and code are available at https://github.com/ZhengyaoFang/CFM.

</details>


### [92] [Agentar-Fin-OCR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11044)
*Siyi Qian,Xiongfei Bai,Bingtao Fu,Yichen Lu,Gaoyang Zhang,Xudong Yang,Peng Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Agentar-Fin-OCR是一个针对金融文档的解析系统，能够将超长金融PDF转换为结构化输出，并引入FinDocBench金融文档基准进行评测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金融文档具有复杂布局、跨页结构不连续和单元格级引用等特定挑战，现有文档解析系统难以处理这些金融领域特有的问题。

Method: 1) 跨页内容整合算法恢复页面连续性，文档级标题层次重建模块构建全局一致的目录树；2) 难度自适应课程学习训练策略用于表格解析，CellBBoxRegressor模块利用结构锚标记定位表格单元格。

Result: 模型在OmniDocBench的表格解析指标上表现优异，并引入FinDocBench基准，包含6类金融文档和专家验证标注，评估指标包括目录编辑距离相似度、跨页拼接TEDS和表格单元格交并比。

Conclusion: Agentar-Fin-OCR和FinDocBench为可靠的金融文档下游应用提供了实用基础，解决了金融领域文档解析的特定挑战。

Abstract: In this paper, we propose Agentar-Fin-OCR, a document parsing system tailored to financial-domain documents, transforming ultra-long financial PDFs into semantically consistent, highly accurate, structured outputs with auditing-grade provenance. To address finance-specific challenges such as complex layouts, cross-page structural discontinuities, and cell-level referencing capability, Agentar-Fin-OCR combines (1) a Cross-page Contents Consolidation algorithm to restore continuity across pages and a Document-level Heading Hierarchy Reconstruction (DHR) module to build a globally consistent Table of Contents (TOC) tree for structure-aware retrieval, and (2) a difficulty-adaptive curriculum learning training strategy for table parsing, together with a CellBBoxRegressor module that uses structural anchor tokens to localize table cells from decoder hidden states without external detectors. Experiments demonstrate that our model shows high performance on the table parsing metrics of OmniDocBench. To enable realistic evaluation in the financial vertical, we further introduce FinDocBench, a benchmark that includes six financial document categories with expert-verified annotations and evaluation metrics including Table of Contents edit-distance-based similarity (TocEDS), cross-page concatenated TEDS, and Table Cell Intersection over Union (C-IoU). We evaluate a wide range of state-of-the-art models on FinDocBench to assess their capabilities and remaining limitations on financial documents. Overall, Agentar-Fin-OCR and FinDocBench provide a practical foundation for reliable downstream financial document applications.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [93] [OmniGuide: Universal Guidance Fields for Enhancing Generalist Robot Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10052)
*Yunzhou Song,Long Le,Yong-Hyun Park,Jie Wang,Junyao Shi,Lingjie Liu,Jiatao Gu,Eric Eaton,Dinesh Jayaraman,Kostas Daniilidis*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: OMNIGUIDE是一个提升视觉-语言-动作模型在复杂任务上性能的框架，通过将多种指导源（如3D基础模型、语义推理VLM、人体姿态模型）表达为可微能量函数来影响动作采样。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前视觉-语言-动作模型在简单任务上表现良好，但在需要复杂空间/语义理解、杂乱环境操作或精确操控的复杂任务上性能有限。需要一种灵活框架来整合多种互补的指导源以提升性能。

Method: 提出OMNIGUIDE框架，将各种指导源（3D基础模型、语义推理VLM、人体姿态模型等）表达为可微能量函数，包含任务特定的吸引子和排斥子，在3D空间中影响VLA模型的动作采样。

Result: 在仿真和真实环境中的大量实验表明，OMNIGUIDE显著提升了最先进通用策略（如π₀.₅、GR00T N1.6）的成功率和安全性。该统一框架匹配或超越了先前为特定指导源设计的专门方法。

Conclusion: OMNIGUIDE提供了一个灵活框架，能够整合多种互补的指导源来提升VLA模型在复杂任务上的性能，为通用智能体的发展提供了重要方向。

Abstract: Vision-language-action(VLA) models have shown great promise as generalist policies for a large range of relatively simple tasks. However, they demonstrate limited performance on more complex tasks, such as those requiring complex spatial or semantic understanding, manipulation in clutter, or precise manipulation. We propose OMNIGUIDE, a flexible framework that improves VLA performance on such tasks by leveraging arbitrary sources of guidance, such as 3D foundation models, semantic-reasoning VLMs, and human pose models. We show how many kinds of guidance can be naturally expressed as differentiable energy functions with task-specific attractors and repellers located in 3D space, that influence the sampling of VLA actions. In this way, OMNIGUIDE enables guidance sources with complementary task-relevant strengths to improve a VLA model's performance on challenging tasks. Extensive experiments in both simulation and real-world environments, across diverse sources of guidance, demonstrate that OMNIGUIDE enhances the performance of state-of-the-art generalist policies (e.g., $π_{0.5}$, GR00T N1.6) significantly across success and safety rates. Critically, our unified framework matches or surpasses the performance of prior methods designed to incorporate specific sources of guidance into VLA policies. Project Page: $\href{https://omniguide.github.io/}{this \; url}$

</details>


### [94] [Model-Free Co-Optimization of Manufacturable Sensor Layouts and Deformation Proprioception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10059)
*Yingjun Tian,Guoxin Fang,Aoran Lyu,Xilong Wang,Zikang Shi,Yuhu Guo,Weiming Wang,Charlie C. L. Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种数据驱动的计算流程，联合优化柔性传感器的数量、长度和布局，以及形状预测网络的参数，用于大自由形变感知


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 柔性传感器在软机器人和可穿戴设备中应用广泛，但现有方法通常通过启发式或试错确定传感器布局，预测精度严重依赖布局设计，缺乏系统化的优化方法

Method: 提出无模型、数据驱动的计算流程，联合优化传感器布局（数量、长度、位置）和形状预测网络参数；使用可微分损失函数同时考虑预测精度和制造约束；无需物理仿真模型，仅依赖形变形状数据集

Result: 通过数值和物理实验验证，该方法相比未优化布局显著提高形变预测精度，同时确保实际可行性；在多种软机器人和可穿戴系统上展示有效性和通用性

Conclusion: 该方法为柔性传感器布局优化提供了一种通用、数据驱动的解决方案，无需物理模型，可广泛应用于各种机器人感知任务，显著提升形变预测性能

Abstract: Flexible sensors are increasingly employed in soft robotics and wearable devices to provide proprioception of freeform deformations.Although supervised learning can train shape predictors from sensor signals, prediction accuracy strongly depends on sensor layout, which is typically determined heuristically or through trial-and-error. This work introduces a model-free, data-driven computational pipeline that jointly optimizes the number, length, and placement of flexible length-measurement sensors together with the parameters of a shape prediction network for large freeform deformations. Unlike model-based approaches, the proposed method relies solely on datasets of deformed shapes, without requiring physical simulation models, and is therefore broadly applicable to diverse robotic sensing tasks. The pipeline incorporates differentiable loss functions that account for both prediction accuracy and manufacturability constraints. By co-optimizing sensor layouts and network parameters, the method significantly improves deformation prediction accuracy over unoptimized layouts while ensuring practical feasibility. The effectiveness and generality of the approach are validated through numerical and physical experiments on multiple soft robotic and wearable systems.

</details>


### [95] [Decision-Aware Uncertainty Evaluation of Vision-Language Model-Based Early Action Anticipation for Human-Robot Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10061)
*Zhaoda Du,Michael Bowman,Qiaojie Zheng,Xiaoli Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文首次系统评估了基于视觉语言模型的短期动作识别在人类-机器人交互中的不确定性，提出了时间前缀评估协议和校准指标，为置信度门控的HRI模块提供了可靠性证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 共享工作空间中的机器人需要从部分、模糊的观察中解释人类动作，但早期过度自信的预测可能导致不安全或破坏性的交互。特别是在自我中心视角下，视角变化和遮挡增加了感知噪声和模糊性，下游HRI模块不仅需要动作假设，还需要在部分观察下可信的置信度估计。

Method: 提出了首个基于视觉语言模型的短期动作识别不确定性系统评估，引入了时间前缀评估协议，以及用于校准和选择性预测的度量标准，并分析了部分观察下的校准错误模式和失效模式。

Result: 研究提供了在时间前缀机制下视觉语言模型不确定性可靠性的缺失证据，表征了部分观察下的校准模式，为在置信度门控的人类-机器人交互模块中使用视觉语言模型预测奠定了基础。

Conclusion: 该研究填补了视觉语言模型在短期动作识别中不确定性评估的空白，为开发更安全、更可靠的基于置信度门控的人类-机器人交互系统提供了关键的可信度证据。

Abstract: Robots in shared workspaces must interpret human actions from partial, ambiguous observations, where overconfident early predictions can lead to unsafe or disruptive interaction. This challenge is amplified in egocentric views, where viewpoint changes and occlusions increase perceptual noise and ambiguity. As a result, downstream human-robot interaction modules require not only an action hypothesis but also a trustworthy estimate of confidence under partial observation. Recent vision-language model-based approaches have been proposed for short-term action recognition due to their open-vocabulary and context-aware reasoning, but their uncertainty reliability in the temporal-prefix regime is largely uncharacterized. We present the first systematic evaluation of uncertainty in vision-language model-based short-term action recognition for human-robot interaction. We introduce a temporal-prefix evaluation protocol and metrics for calibration and selective prediction. We also characterize miscalibration patterns and failure modes under partial observations. Our study provides the missing reliability evidence needed to use vision-language model predictions in confidence-gated human-robot interaction modules.

</details>


### [96] [AR-VLA: True Autoregressive Action Expert for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10126)
*Yutong Hu,Jan-Nico Zaech,Nikolay Nikolov,Yuanqi Yao,Sombit Dey,Giuliano Albanese,Renaud Detry,Luc Van Gool,Danda Paudel*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出AR-VLA：一种自回归动作专家模型，通过长时记忆和重新锚定机制实现上下文感知的连续动作生成，解决视觉-语言-动作模态间的频率不匹配问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型和扩散策略存在两个主要问题：1）每次新观察都会重置时间上下文，只能被动反应式预测动作；2）快速控制与慢速推理之间存在频率不匹配。需要一种能保持历史上下文、实现时空一致动作生成的架构。

Method: 提出自回归动作专家，将动作生成为连续因果序列，同时以可刷新的视觉-语言前缀为条件。通过长时记忆保持历史上下文，使用重新锚定机制同步异步的视觉-语言-动作模态，数学上考虑感知延迟。支持运动学语法的独立预训练和与重型感知骨干的模块化集成。

Result: 在模拟和真实机器人操作任务上的实验表明，AR-VLA能有效替代传统的基于块的动作头，适用于专家和通用策略。相比最先进的反应式VLA，AR-VLA展现出更好的历史感知能力、更平滑的动作轨迹，同时保持或超过任务成功率。

Conclusion: 提出了一个可扩展的、上下文感知的动作生成框架，为训练有效的机器人策略提供了稳健的结构基础。AR-VLA通过自回归动作专家和重新锚定机制，解决了VLA模型中的频率不匹配和上下文一致性问题。

Abstract: We propose a standalone autoregressive (AR) Action Expert that generates actions as a continuous causal sequence while conditioning on refreshable vision-language prefixes. In contrast to existing Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models and diffusion policies that reset temporal context with each new observation and predict actions reactively, our Action Expert maintains its own history through a long-lived memory and is inherently context-aware. This structure addresses the frequency mismatch between fast control and slow reasoning, enabling efficient independent pretraining of kinematic syntax and modular integration with heavy perception backbones, naturally ensuring spatio-temporally consistent action generation across frames. To synchronize these asynchronous hybrid V-L-A modalities, we utilize a re-anchoring mechanism that mathematically accounts for perception staleness during both training and inference. Experiments on simulated and real-robot manipulation tasks demonstrate that the proposed method can effectively replace traditional chunk-based action heads for both specialist and generalist policies. AR-VLA exhibits superior history awareness and substantially smoother action trajectories while maintaining or exceeding the task success rates of state-of-the-art reactive VLAs. Overall, our work introduces a scalable, context-aware action generation schema that provides a robust structural foundation for training effective robotic policies.

</details>


### [97] [Cross-Hand Latent Representation for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10158)
*Guangqi Jiang,Yutong Liang,Jianglong Ye,Jia-Yang Huang,Changwei Jing,Rocky Duan,Pieter Abbeel,Xiaolong Wang,Xueyan Zou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: XL-VLA是一个跨具身视觉-语言-动作框架，通过统一的潜在动作空间实现不同灵巧手之间的数据共享和迁移学习，解决了灵巧操作中数据收集成本高、新具身快速出现的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 灵巧操作对机器人自主性至关重要，但训练可靠的视觉-语言-动作模型需要大规模演示数据。随着新灵巧手具身的快速出现，为每个具身收集数据成本高昂且不切实际，需要可扩展的跨具身学习方法。

Method: 引入XL-VLA框架，集成一个跨不同灵巧手共享的统一潜在动作空间。这个具身不变的潜在空间可直接插入标准VLA架构，实现无缝的跨具身训练和现有/新收集数据的高效重用。

Result: 实验结果表明，XL-VLA在原始关节空间上持续优于基线VLA模型，证明其作为可扩展跨具身灵巧操作的有效解决方案。

Conclusion: XL-VLA通过统一的潜在动作空间解决了跨具身灵巧操作的数据可扩展性问题，为不同机器人手的视觉-语言-动作学习提供了高效框架。

Abstract: Dexterous manipulation is essential for real-world robot autonomy, mirroring the central role of human hand coordination in daily activity. Humans rely on rich multimodal perception--vision, sound, and language-guided intent--to perform dexterous actions, motivating vision-based, language-conditioned manipulation systems for robots. However, training reliable vision-language-action (VLA) models for dexterous manipulation requires large-scale demonstrations across many robotic hands. In addition, as new dexterous embodiments appear rapidly, collecting data for each becomes costly and impractical, creating a need for scalable cross-embodiment learning. We introduce XL-VLA, a vision-language-action framework integrated with a unified latent action space shared across diverse dexterous hands. This embodiment-invariant latent space is directly pluggable into standard VLA architectures, enabling seamless cross-embodiment training and efficient reuse of both existing and newly collected data. Experimental results demonstrate that XL-VLA consistently outperforms baseline VLA models operating in raw joint spaces, establishing it as an effective solution for scalable cross-embodiment dexterous manipulation.

</details>


### [98] [Dance2Hesitate: A Multi-Modal Dataset of Dancer-Taught Hesitancy for Understandable Robot Motion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10166)
*Srikrishna Bangalore Raghu,Anna Soukhovei,Divya Sai Sindhuja Vankineni,Alexandra Bacula,Alessandro Roncone*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出并开源了一个多模态、舞者生成的犹豫运动数据集，用于研究人机协作中机器人犹豫表达对协调策略和安全判断的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在人机协作中，机器人表达犹豫是影响人类协调策略、注意力分配和安全判断的关键因素。然而，设计具有泛化能力的犹豫机器人运动具有挑战性，因为观察者的推断高度依赖于具体形态和上下文环境。

Method: 创建了一个多模态数据集，包含：(1) 在Franka Emika Panda机械臂上通过示教演示到达Jenga塔的三种犹豫程度（轻微、显著、极端）运动；(2) 同步RGB-D运动捕捉的舞者表演相同到达行为的三种犹豫程度上肢运动，以及极端犹豫程度的全身运动序列。

Result: 获得了70个独特的全身轨迹、84个跨越三种犹豫程度的上肢轨迹，以及66个跨越三种犹豫程度的示教轨迹。提供了可重复基准测试的文档，数据集已开源。

Conclusion: 该数据集为解决犹豫运动设计的泛化挑战提供了重要资源，特别关注特定上下文-形态对，为人机协作中自然犹豫表达的研究奠定了基础。

Abstract: In human-robot collaboration, a robot's expression of hesitancy is a critical factor that shapes human coordination strategies, attention allocation, and safety-related judgments. However, designing hesitant robot motion that generalizes is challenging because the observer's inference is highly dependent on embodiment and context. To address these challenges, we introduce and open-source a multi-modal, dancer-generated dataset of hesitant motion where we focus on specific context-embodiment pairs (i.e., manipulator/human upper-limb approaching a Jenga Tower, and anthropomorphic whole body motion in free space). The dataset includes (i) kinesthetic teaching demonstrations on a Franka Emika Panda reaching from a fixed start configuration to a fixed target (a Jenga tower) with three graded hesitancy levels (slight, significant, extreme) and (ii) synchronized RGB-D motion capture of dancers performing the same reaching behavior using their upper limb across three hesitancy levels, plus full human body sequences for extreme hesitancy. We further provide documentation to enable reproducible benchmarking across robot and human modalities. Across all dancers, we obtained 70 unique whole-body trajectories, 84 upper limb trajectories spanning over the three hesitancy levels, and 66 kinesthetic teaching trajectories spanning over the three hesitancy levels. The dataset can be accessed here: https://brsrikrishna.github.io/Dance2Hesitate/.

</details>


### [99] [Characterizing Healthy & Post-Stroke Neuromotor Behavior During 6D Upper-Limb Isometric Gaming: Implications for Design of End-Effector Rehabilitation Robot Interfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10173)
*Ajay Anand,Gabriel Parra,Chad A. Berghoff,Laura A. Hallock*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究利用开源数据集分析健康和中风后用户在康复机器人上的运动行为，发现界面设计细节影响用户行为，病理特征可从6D力数据中检测，并提出基于HMM的神经运动行为分类方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人辅助康复需要设计能促进健康运动练习的游戏和机器人干预，但由于用户神经运动行为、游戏界面和物理机器人之间的复杂相互作用，使得系统设计以及区分"健康"与病理行为变得困难。

Method: 使用OpenRobotRehab 1.0开源数据集，分析13名健康用户和2名中风后用户在末端执行器康复机器人上执行等距轨迹跟踪任务时的力输出、肌肉激活和游戏表现。提出基于表面肌电信号的隐马尔可夫模型神经运动行为分类方法。

Result: 任务规范（包括约束轴和用户对跟踪指令的解释）影响用户行为；病理相关特征可在等距任务执行的6D末端执行器力数据中检测到（健康和中风后用户在力误差和平均力产生方面有显著差异，p=0.05）；健康神经运动策略具有异质性且难以表征；HMM模型能区分健康和中风后的神经运动动态，而基于协同作用的分解方法无法实现这种区分。

Conclusion: 研究结果为设计能够促进不同用户群体健康运动策略的自适应末端执行器康复机器人提供了重要启示，强调了界面设计细节的重要性，并展示了基于HMM的方法在神经运动行为分类中的潜力。

Abstract: Successful robot-mediated rehabilitation requires designing games and robot interventions that promote healthy motor practice. However, the interplay between a given user's neuromotor behavior, the gaming interface, and the physical robot makes designing system elements -- and even characterizing what behaviors are "healthy" or pathological -- challenging. We leverage our OpenRobotRehab 1.0 open access data set to assess the characteristics of 13 healthy and 2 post-stroke users' force output, muscle activations, and game performance while executing isometric trajectory tracking tasks using an end-effector rehabilitation robot. We present an assessment of how subtle aspects of interface design impact user behavior; an analysis of how pathological neuromotor behaviors are reflected in end-effector force dynamics; and a novel hidden Markov model (HMM)-based neuromotor behavior classification method based on surface electromyography (sEMG) signals during cyclic motions. We demonstrate that task specification (including which axes are constrained and how users interpret tracking instructions) shapes user behavior; that pathology-related features are detectable in 6D end-effector force data during isometric task execution (with significant differences between healthy and post-stroke profiles in force error and average force production at $p=0.05$); and that healthy neuromotor strategies are heterogeneous and inherently difficult to characterize. We also show that our HMM-based models discriminate healthy and post-stroke neuromotor dynamics where synergy-based decompositions reflect no such differentiation. Lastly, we discuss these results' implications for the design of adaptive end-effector rehabilitation robots capable of promoting healthier movement strategies across diverse user populations.

</details>


### [100] [Autonomous Search for Sparsely Distributed Visual Phenomena through Environmental Context Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10174)
*Eric Chen,Travis Manderson,Nare Karapetyan,Peter Edmunds,Nicholas Roy,Yogesh Girdhar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出使用视觉环境上下文（目标物种常出现的栖息地特征）作为信号，结合单样本检测和自适应上下文建模，提高AUV在稀疏分布珊瑚物种搜索中的效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主水下航行器(AUV)用于珊瑚礁调查时，难以高效定位稀疏分布的目标物种。由于电池寿命有限，AUV无法全面搜索所有区域，而目标检测本身过于稀疏无法提供方向指导。

Method: 使用单张标注图像，通过patch级DINOv2嵌入进行在线单样本检测，同时检测目标物种及其周围环境上下文。利用环境上下文特征（比目标检测更密集、变化更平滑）构建奖励函数，使自适应规划器能做出更好的搜索决策。

Result: 在美属维尔京群岛圣约翰两个珊瑚礁站点的真实AUV图像数据上进行离线模拟验证。结果显示，单样本检测结合自适应上下文建模能实现高效自主调查：当目标稀疏分布时，能在约一半时间内采样到75%的目标，优于仅使用目标检测的搜索策略。

Conclusion: 利用视觉环境上下文作为额外信号，结合单样本检测和自适应规划，能显著提高AUV在稀疏分布珊瑚物种搜索中的效率，优于传统覆盖式搜索和仅依赖目标检测的策略。

Abstract: Autonomous underwater vehicles (AUVs) are increasingly used to survey coral reefs, yet efficiently locating specific coral species of interest remains difficult: target species are often sparsely distributed across the reef, and an AUV with limited battery life cannot afford to search everywhere. When detections of the target itself are too sparse to provide directional guidance, the robot benefits from an additional signal to decide where to look next. We propose using the visual environmental context -- the habitat features that tend to co-occur with a target species -- as that signal. Because context features are spatially denser and often vary more smoothly than target detections, we hypothesize that a reward function targeted at broader environmental context will enable adaptive planners to make better decisions on where to go next, even in regions where no target has yet been observed. Starting from a single labeled image, our method uses patch-level DINOv2 embeddings to perform one-shot detections of both the target species and its surrounding context online. We validate our approach using real imagery collected by an AUV at two reef sites in St. John, U.S. Virgin Islands, simulating the robot's motion offline. Our results demonstrate that one-shot detection combined with adaptive context modeling enables efficient autonomous surveying, sampling up to 75$\%$ of the target in roughly half the time required by exhaustive coverage when the target is sparsely distributed, and outperforming search strategies that only use target detections.

</details>


### [101] [Octopus-inspired Distributed Control for Soft Robotic Arms: A Graph Neural Network-Based Attention Policy with Environmental Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10198)
*Linxin Hou,Qirui Wu,Zhihang Qin,Yongxin Guo,Cecilia Laschi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SoftGM：一种受章鱼启发的分布式控制架构，用于分段软体机械臂，通过在线障碍物发现学习在接触丰富的环境中到达目标，无需全局障碍物几何信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软体机械臂在复杂环境中操作时面临挑战，特别是需要适应未知障碍物和接触交互。现有方法通常依赖全局环境信息，而实际应用中障碍物几何信息往往是未知的。需要一种能够在线发现障碍物并协调多段臂节的控制架构。

Method: 1. 将每个臂节建模为合作智能体，将臂-环境交互表示为图结构；2. 采用两阶段图注意力消息传递方案；3. 遵循集中训练分散执行（CTDE）范式，使用集中式评论家和分散式执行器；4. 在Cosserat-rod模拟器（PyElastica）中实现。

Result: 在三个复杂度递增的任务中评估：无障碍物、结构化障碍物、带孔墙壁场景。与六种主流多智能体强化学习基线相比，SoftGM在简单设置中与强CTDE方法相当，在带孔墙壁任务中表现最佳。在观测噪声、单节驱动故障和瞬态干扰的鲁棒性测试中，SoftGM保持成功率同时控制努力有界。

Conclusion: SoftGM通过选择性接触相关信息路由驱动的弹性协调，实现了在接触丰富环境中无需全局障碍物几何信息的有效目标到达。该架构展示了在复杂环境中软体机械臂控制的鲁棒性和适应性。

Abstract: This paper proposes SoftGM, an octopus-inspired distributed control architecture for segmented soft robotic arms that learn to reach targets in contact-rich environments using online obstacle discovery without relying on global obstacle geometry. SoftGM formulates each arm section as a cooperative agent and represents the arm-environment interaction as a graph. SoftGM uses a two-stage graph attention message passing scheme following a Centralised Training Decentralised Execution (CTDE) paradigm with a centralised critic and decentralised actor. We evaluate SoftGM in a Cosserat-rod simulator (PyElastica) across three tasks that increase the complexity of the environment: obstacle-free, structured obstacles, and a wall-with-hole scenario. Compared with six widely used MARL baselines (IDDPG, IPPO, ISAC, MADDPG, MAPPO, MASAC) under identical information content and training conditions, SoftGM matches strong CTDE methods in simpler settings and achieves the best performance in the wall-with-hole task. Robustness tests with observation noise, single-section actuation failure, and transient disturbances show that SoftGM preserves success while keeping control effort bounded, indicating resilient coordination driven by selective contact-relevant information routing.

</details>


### [102] [Perceptive Hierarchical-Task MPC for Sequential Mobile Manipulation in Unstructured Semi-Static Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10227)
*Xintong Du,Jingxing Qian,Siqi Zhou,Angela P. Schoellig*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出感知分层任务模型预测控制框架，用于非结构化变化环境中的顺序移动操作，通过贝叶斯推理建模物体级变化，实现高效任务执行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 顺序移动操作面临独特挑战：机器人长时间操作时，成功不仅依赖一致的运动生成，还需要对操作环境变化的感知和适应。现有运动规划器通常假设环境静态且依赖预计算地图，这在长期操作中常因半静态变化（如物体移除、引入或移动）而失效。

Method: 提出感知分层任务模型预测控制框架：1）使用贝叶斯推理框架显式建模物体级变化，维护时间准确的3D环境表示；2）将最新环境表示嵌入词典优化框架，实现顺序任务的高效执行。

Result: 通过仿真和真实机器人实验验证：相比基线方法，该方法系统性地考虑移动和幻影障碍物，以更高效率和反应性成功完成顺序任务，无需先验地图或外部基础设施。

Conclusion: 提出的感知HTMPC框架能够有效处理非结构化变化环境中的顺序移动操作，通过显式建模环境变化和词典优化，实现了对动态环境的适应性和任务执行效率的提升。

Abstract: As compared to typical mobile manipulation tasks, sequential mobile manipulation poses a unique challenge -- as the robot operates over extended periods, successful task completion is not solely dependent on consistent motion generation but also on the robot's awareness and adaptivity to changes in the operating environment. While existing motion planners can generate whole-body trajectories to complete sequential tasks, they typically assume that the environment remains static and rely on precomputed maps. This assumption often breaks down during long-term operations, where semi-static changes such as object removal, introduction, or shifts are common. In this work, we propose a novel perceptive hierarchical-task model predictive control (HTMPC) framework for efficient sequential mobile manipulation in unstructured, changing environments. To tackle the challenge, we leverage a Bayesian inference framework to explicitly model object-level changes and thereby maintain a temporally accurate representation of the 3D environment; this up-to-date representation is embedded in a lexicographic optimization framework to enable efficient execution of sequential tasks. We validate our perceptive HTMPC approach through both simulated and real-robot experiments. In contrast to baseline methods, our approach systematically accounts for moved and phantom obstacles, successfully completing sequential tasks with higher efficiency and reactivity, without relying on prior maps or external infrastructure.

</details>


### [103] [Hierarchical Task Model Predictive Control for Sequential Mobile Manipulation Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10232)
*Xintong Du,Siqi Zhou,Angela P. Schoellig*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于非线性词典优化的分层任务模型预测控制框架，用于移动机械臂顺序任务执行，相比现有方法性能提升显著


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动机械臂在复杂日常任务中需要更好的决策算法，以衔接高层任务规划器并高效执行任务序列

Method: 基于非线性词典优化的分层任务模型预测控制框架，有效利用机器人冗余度

Result: 相比任务优先逆运动学控制方法，任务切换、奇异点和参考变化时的分层轨迹跟踪性能平均提升42%；相比单任务架构，任务空间路径更短，执行时间快2.3倍

Conclusion: 提出的分层任务控制框架能显著提升移动机械臂顺序任务执行的性能和反应性，在9自由度移动机械臂上验证有效

Abstract: Mobile manipulators are envisioned to serve more complex roles in people's everyday lives. With recent breakthroughs in large language models, task planners have become better at translating human verbal instructions into a sequence of tasks. However, there is still a need for a decision-making algorithm that can seamlessly interface with the high-level task planner to carry out the sequence of tasks efficiently. In this work, building on the idea of nonlinear lexicographic optimization, we propose a novel Hierarchical-Task Model Predictive Control framework that is able to complete sequential tasks with improved performance and reactivity by effectively leveraging the robot's redundancy. Compared to the state-of-the-art task-prioritized inverse kinematic control method, our approach has improved hierarchical trajectory tracking performance by 42% on average when facing task changes, robot singularity and reference variations. Compared to a typical single-task architecture, our proposed hierarchical task control architecture enables the robot to traverse a shorter path in task space and achieves an execution time 2.3 times faster when executing a sequence of delivery tasks. We demonstrated the results with real-world experiments on a 9 degrees of freedom mobile manipulator.

</details>


### [104] [Degeneracy-Resilient Teach and Repeat for Geometrically Challenging Environments Using FMCW Lidar](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10248)
*Katya M. Papais,Wenda Zhao,Timothy D. Barfoot*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于FMCW激光雷达的抗退化T&R导航系统，通过多普勒速度里程计和退化感知扫描到地图定位，在几何退化环境中实现可靠自主导航。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于ICP的T&R导航系统在几何退化环境（如平坦地形）中容易变得病态，导致定位性能下降和导航不可靠。需要一种能在稀疏结构环境中稳定工作的导航方法。

Method: 1. 利用FMCW激光雷达的多普勒效应获取每点的径向速度测量；2. 开发几何无关、无对应关系的运动估计方法，包含稳定的位姿不确定性估计；3. 提出退化感知定位方法，结合每点曲率改进数据关联，统一平移和旋转尺度以实现一致的退化检测。

Result: 在三种不同结构丰富度的环境中进行闭环现场实验，证明该系统能可靠完成自主导航，包括在平坦机场测试场等挑战性环境中，而传统基于ICP的系统在此类环境中会失败。

Conclusion: 提出的FMCW激光雷达T&R导航系统通过多普勒速度里程计和退化感知定位，有效解决了几何退化环境中的导航问题，为GPS拒止环境下的可靠自主导航提供了解决方案。

Abstract: Teach and Repeat (T&R) topometric navigation enables robots to autonomously repeat previously traversed paths without relying on GPS, making it well suited for operations in GPS-denied environments such as underground mines and lunar navigation. State-of-the-art T&R systems typically rely on iterative closest point (ICP)-based estimation; however, in geometrically degenerate environments with sparsely structured terrain, ICP often becomes ill-conditioned, resulting in degraded localization and unreliable navigation performance. To address this challenge, we present a degeneracy-resilient Frequency-Modulated Continuous-Wave (FMCW) lidar T&R navigation system consisting of Doppler velocity-based odometry and degeneracy-aware scan-to-map localization. Leveraging FMCW lidar, which provides per-point radial velocity measurements via the Doppler effect, we extend a geometry-independent, correspondence-free motion estimation to include principled pose uncertainty estimation that remains stable in degenerate environments. We further propose a degeneracy-aware localization method that incorporates per-point curvature for improved data association, and unifies translational and rotational scales to enable consistent degeneracy detection. Closed-loop field experiments across three environments with varying structural richness demonstrate that the proposed system reliably completes autonomous navigation, including in a challenging flat airport test field where a conventional ICP-based system fails.

</details>


### [105] [From Prior to Pro: Efficient Skill Mastery via Distribution Contractive RL Finetuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10263)
*Zhanyi Sun,Shuran Song*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DICE-RL是一个使用强化学习作为"分布收缩"算子的框架，通过在线反馈放大高成功率行为，将预训练的生成式机器人策略转化为高性能策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有预训练的生成式机器人策略虽然具有广泛的行为覆盖，但性能不够优化。需要一种方法能够利用在线反馈来提升策略性能，同时保持稳定性和样本效率。

Method: 首先预训练基于扩散或流的策略以获得广泛行为覆盖，然后使用结合选择性行为正则化和价值引导动作选择的残差离策略RL框架进行微调。

Result: DICE-RL在模拟和真实机器人上都能可靠提升性能，具有强稳定性和样本效率，能够直接从高维像素输入掌握复杂的长期操作技能。

Conclusion: DICE-RL框架成功地将强化学习作为分布收缩算子，能够有效地将预训练的行为先验转化为高性能策略，为机器人技能学习提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: We introduce Distribution Contractive Reinforcement Learning (DICE-RL), a framework that uses reinforcement learning (RL) as a "distribution contraction" operator to refine pretrained generative robot policies. DICE-RL turns a pretrained behavior prior into a high-performing "pro" policy by amplifying high-success behaviors from online feedback. We pretrain a diffusion- or flow-based policy for broad behavioral coverage, then finetune it with a stable, sample-efficient residual off-policy RL framework that combines selective behavior regularization with value-guided action selection. Extensive experiments and analyses show that DICE-RL reliably improves performance with strong stability and sample efficiency. It enables mastery of complex long-horizon manipulation skills directly from high-dimensional pixel inputs, both in simulation and on a real robot. Project website: https://zhanyisun.github.io/dice.rl.2026/.

</details>


### [106] [Design of a Robot-Assisted Chemical Dialysis System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10264)
*Diane Jung,Caleb Escobedo,Noah Liska,Maitrey Gramopadhye,Daniel Szafir,Alessandro Roncone,Carson Bruns*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 研究人员开发了一个机器人辅助透析系统，通过用户中心设计方法减少科学家在长时间实验中的工作负担


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学家需要执行各种繁琐、耗时的实验操作，这些手动程序成为现代实验科学的瓶颈，消耗时间并增加材料科学和医学等领域的工作负担

Method: 采用用户中心设计方法，通过两次可用性研究获取参与者反馈，修订设计需求，开发最终满足科学家需求的机器人辅助透析系统

Result: 开发出满足科学家需求的最终系统，该系统有潜力应用于其他实验工作流程，减少科学家在长时间实验程序中的工作负担

Conclusion: 该系统有望加速科学发现，将科学家从繁琐劳动中解放出来，为设计更多类似系统提供了有效方法

Abstract: Scientists perform diverse manual procedures that are tedious and laborious. Such procedures are considered a bottleneck for modern experimental science, as they consume time and increase burdens in fields including material science and medicine. We employ a user-centered approach to designing a robot-assisted system for dialysis, a common multi-day purification method used in polymer and protein synthesis. Through two usability studies, we obtain participant feedback and revise design requirements to develop the final system that satisfies scientists' needs and has the potential for applications in other experimental workflows. We anticipate that integration of this system into real synthesis procedures in a chemical wet lab will decrease workload on scientists during long experimental procedures and provide an effective approach to designing more systems that have the potential to accelerate scientific discovery and liberate scientists from tedious labor.

</details>


### [107] [Update-Free On-Policy Steering via Verifiers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10282)
*Maria Attarian,Ian Vyse,Claas Voelcker,Jasper Gerigk,Evgenii Opryshko,Anas Almasri,Sumeet Singh,Yilun Du,Igor Gilitschenski*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: UF-OPS：一种无需更新的在线策略引导方法，通过验证器预测动作成功概率，引导基础策略选择更高成功率的动作，提升行为克隆策略的鲁棒性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 行为克隆（BC）策略虽然流行，但通常脆弱且难以处理精确操作任务，需要一种轻量级方法来提升现有策略的性能而不改变其参数

Method: 训练验证器函数来预测动作成功概率，使用策略评估期间获得的滚动数据，在执行时引导基础策略选择更高成功率的动作，无需更新基础策略参数

Result: 在仿真和真实世界实验中，相比基础策略，在5个真实任务上平均成功率提升49%，对黑盒扩散策略有效

Conclusion: UF-OPS是一种轻量级、灵活的方法，能够显著提升现有行为克隆策略的性能，无需修改基础策略参数，适用于实际机器人应用

Abstract: In recent years, Behavior Cloning (BC) has become one of the most prevalent methods for enabling robots to mimic human demonstrations. However, despite their successes, BC policies are often brittle and struggle with precise manipulation. To overcome these issues, we propose UF-OPS, an Update-Free On-Policy Steering method that enables the robot to predict the success likelihood of its actions and adapt its strategy at execution time. We accomplish this by training verifier functions using policy rollout data obtained during an initial evaluation of the policy. These verifiers are subsequently used to steer the base policy toward actions with a higher likelihood of success. Our method improves the performance of black-box diffusion policy, without changing the base parameters, making it light-weight and flexible. We present results from both simulation and real-world data and achieve an average 49% improvement in success rate over the base policy across 5 real tasks.

</details>


### [108] [SteadyTray: Learning Object Balancing Tasks in Humanoid Tray Transport via Residual Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10306)
*Anlun Huang,Zhenyu Wu,Soofiyan Atar,Yuheng Zhi,Michael Yip*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ReST-RL：分层强化学习架构，将双足机器人的运动与负载稳定解耦，通过残差模块主动抵消步态扰动，实现稳定托盘运输


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态双足运动固有的振荡使得未固定负载难以稳定运输，这是人形机器人在非结构化环境中的关键工程瓶颈

Method: 分层强化学习架构：1）鲁棒的基础运动策略；2）动态残差模块，专门设计用于主动抵消末端执行器处的步态诱导扰动。这种架构分离确保稳定托盘运输而不损害底层双足稳定性

Result: 在仿真中显著优于端到端基线：步态平滑度和方向精度更高，变速度跟踪成功率96.9%，外力扰动鲁棒性74.5%。成功部署于Unitree G1人形机器人硬件，在各种物体和外部力扰动下实现零样本仿真到现实的泛化

Conclusion: 模块化分层方法有效解决了双足机器人负载稳定问题，实现了可靠的仿真到现实迁移，为动态环境中的人形机器人应用提供了实用解决方案

Abstract: Stabilizing unsecured payloads against the inherent oscillations of dynamic bipedal locomotion remains a critical engineering bottleneck for humanoids in unstructured environments. To solve this, we introduce ReST-RL, a hierarchical reinforcement learning architecture that explicitly decouples locomotion from payload stabilization, evaluated via the SteadyTray benchmark. Rather than relying on monolithic end-to-end learning, our framework integrates a robust base locomotion policy with a dynamic residual module engineered to actively cancel gait-induced perturbations at the end-effector. This architectural separation ensures steady tray transport without degrading the underlying bipedal stability. In simulation, the residual design significantly outperforms end-to-end baselines in gait smoothness and orientation accuracy, achieving a 96.9% success rate in variable velocity tracking and 74.5% robustness against external force disturbances. Successfully deployed on the Unitree G1 humanoid hardware, this modular approach demonstrates highly reliable zero-shot sim-to-real generalization across various objects and external force disturbances.

</details>


### [109] [PC-Diffuser: Path-Consistent Capsule CBF Safety Filtering for Diffusion-Based Trajectory Planner](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10330)
*Eugene Ku,Yiwei Lyu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PC-Diffuser：一种将可认证的安全屏障函数结构嵌入扩散规划去噪循环的安全增强框架，使安全成为轨迹生成的内在部分而非后处理


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂交通中的自动驾驶需要超越手工规则的规划器，但基于扩散的轨迹规划器虽然表现出色，却难以认证且在罕见或分布外场景中可能灾难性失败

Method: 将可认证的路径一致屏障函数结构直接嵌入扩散规划的去噪循环中，使用胶囊距离屏障函数评估碰撞风险，将去噪路径点转换为动态可行的运动，应用路径一致安全过滤器消除约束违规

Result: PC-Diffuser实现了迭代的、上下文感知的安全保障，而不是后处理修复，使修正后的计划保持接近学习分布

Conclusion: 通过将安全一致修正注入每个去噪步骤并将精炼轨迹反馈到扩散过程中，PC-Diffuser解决了扩散规划的安全认证问题

Abstract: Autonomous driving in complex traffic requires planners that generalize beyond hand-crafted rules, motivating data-driven approaches that learn behavior from expert demonstrations. Diffusion-based trajectory planners have recently shown strong closed-loop performance by iteratively denoising a full-horizon plan, but they remain difficult to certify and can fail catastrophically in rare or out-of-distribution scenarios. To address this challenge, we present PC-Diffuser, a safety augmentation framework that embeds a certifiable, path-consistent barrier-function structure directly into the denoising loop of diffusion planning. The key idea is to make safety an intrinsic part of trajectory generation rather than a post-hoc fix: we enforce forward invariance along the rollout while preserving the diffusion model's intended path geometry. Specifically, PC-Diffuser (i) evaluates collision risk using a capsule-distance barrier function that better reflects vehicle geometry and reduces unnecessary conservativeness, (ii) converts denoised waypoints into dynamically feasible motion under a kinematic bicycle model, and (iii) applies a path-consistent safety filter that eliminates residual constraint violations without geometric distortion, so the corrected plan remains close to the learned distribution. By injecting these safety-consistent corrections at every denoising step and feeding the refined trajectory back into the diffusion process, PC-Diffuser enables iterative, context-aware safeguarding instead of post-hoc repair...

</details>


### [110] [Adaptive Manipulation Potential and Haptic Estimation for Tool-Mediated Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10352)
*Lin Yang,Anirvan Dutta,Yuan Ji,Yanxin Zhou,Shilin Shan,Lv Chen,Etienne Burdet,Domenico Campolo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于参数化平衡流形的工具操作框架，整合触觉估计、在线规划和自适应刚度控制，通过触觉SLAM和不确定性感知阻抗控制实现鲁棒的工具操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决接触丰富的工具操作中视觉遮挡和触觉感知不确定性的挑战，实现人类水平的灵巧操作能力。

Method: 1) 引入参数化平衡流形作为工具交互的统一表示；2) 建立物理-几何对偶性，使用包含可微接触模型的自适应操作势能；3) 将触觉估计重构为流形参数估计问题，采用混合推理策略（触觉SLAM）；4) 通过连续更新操作势能参数动态重塑平衡流形，指导在线轨迹重规划和不确定性感知阻抗控制。

Result: 在仿真和超过260次真实世界螺丝松动实验中验证了系统有效性。实验结果显示在标准场景中实现了鲁棒的识别和操作成功，同时保持准确跟踪。消融研究证实触觉SLAM和不确定性感知刚度调制优于固定阻抗基线，有效防止紧密公差交互中的卡滞。

Conclusion: 提出的平衡流形框架成功整合了触觉感知和自适应控制，为工具介导的操作提供了统一的表示和闭环解决方案，显著提升了在视觉遮挡和触觉不确定性条件下的操作性能。

Abstract: Achieving human-level dexterity in contact-rich, tool-mediated manipulation remains a significant challenge due to visual occlusion and the underdetermined nature of haptic sensing. This paper introduces a parameterized Equilibrium Manifold (EM) as a unified representation for tool-mediated interaction, and develops a closed-loop framework that integrates haptic estimation, online planning, and adaptive stiffness control. We establish a physical-geometric duality using an adaptive manipulation potential incorporating a differentiable contact model, which induces the manifold's geometric structure and ensures that complex physical interactions are encapsulated as continuous operations on the EM. Within this framework, we reformulate haptic estimation as a manifold parameter estimation problem. Specifically, a hybrid inference strategy (haptic SLAM) is employed in which discrete object shapes are classified via particle filtering, while the continuous object pose is estimated using analytical gradients for efficient optimization. By continuously updating the parameters of the manipulation potential, the framework dynamically reshapes the induced EM to guide online trajectory replanning and implement uncertainty-aware impedance control, thereby closing the perception-action loop. The system is validated through simulation and over 260 real-world screw-loosening trials. Experimental results demonstrate robust identification and manipulation success in standard scenarios while maintaining accurate tracking. Furthermore, ablation studies confirm that haptic SLAM and uncertainty-aware stiffness modulation outperform fixed impedance baselines, effectively preventing jamming during tight tolerance interactions.

</details>


### [111] [Few-Shot Adaptation to Non-Stationary Environments via Latent Trend Embedding for Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10373)
*Yasuyuki Fujii,Emika Kameda,Hiroki Fukada,Yoshiki Mori,Tadashi Matsuo,Nobutaka Shimada*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于潜在趋势ID的少样本适应框架，通过估计低维环境状态而非修改模型参数来应对概念漂移，避免灾难性遗忘并降低计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人系统在现实环境中常面临概念漂移问题，传统适应方法通过更新模型参数会导致灾难性遗忘且计算成本高，需要一种更高效、可扩展的适应方案。

Method: 提出潜在趋势ID框架：1）通过反向传播估计低维环境状态（趋势ID），保持模型参数固定；2）引入时间正则化和状态转移模型，确保潜在空间平滑演化，防止过拟合。

Result: 在定量食物抓取任务实验中，学习到的趋势ID在潜在空间中分布在不同的区域，具有时间一致的轨迹，能够在不修改模型参数的情况下实现少样本适应到未见环境。

Conclusion: 该框架为在多样化和演化环境中运行的机器人应用提供了可扩展且可解释的解决方案，通过估计环境状态而非修改模型权重来实现高效适应。

Abstract: Robotic systems operating in real-world environments often suffer from concept shift, where the input-output relationship changes due to latent environmental factors that are not directly observable. Conventional adaptation methods update model parameters, which may cause catastrophic forgetting and incur high computational cost. This paper proposes a latent Trend ID-based framework for few-shot adaptation in non-stationary environments. Instead of modifying model weights, a low-dimensional environmental state, referred to as the Trend ID, is estimated via backpropagation while the model parameters remain fixed. To prevent overfitting caused by per-sample latent variables, we introduce temporal regularization and a state transition model that enforces smooth evolution of the latent space. Experiments on a quantitative food grasping task demonstrate that the learned Trend IDs are distributed across distinct regions of the latent space with temporally consistent trajectories, and that few-shot adaptation to unseen environments is achieved without modifying model parameters. The proposed framework provides a scalable and interpretable solution for robotics applications operating across diverse and evolving environments.

</details>


### [112] [ScanDP: Generalizable 3D Scanning with Diffusion Policy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10390)
*Itsuki Hirako,Ryo Hakoda,Yubin Liu,Matthew Hwang,Yoshihiro Sato,Takeshi Oishi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于扩散策略的数据高效3D扫描框架，模仿人类扫描策略，使用占据栅格映射提升鲁棒性，结合球体空间表示和路径优化，在未见物体上实现更高覆盖率和更短路径


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于强化学习的3D扫描方法需要大量训练数据且难以泛化到未见物体类别，需要更数据高效且泛化能力强的扫描框架

Method: 使用扩散策略模仿人类扫描策略；采用占据栅格映射替代直接点云处理以提升噪声鲁棒性；结合球体空间表示和路径优化确保路径安全和扫描效率

Result: 在形状和尺度各异的未见物体上评估，相比基线方法获得更高覆盖率和更短路径，对传感器噪声保持鲁棒，实际执行中表现稳定可行

Conclusion: 提出的数据高效3D扫描框架通过扩散策略和占据栅格映射，实现了对未见物体的有效泛化，在覆盖率和路径效率上优于现有方法，具有实际应用价值

Abstract: Learning-based 3D Scanning plays a crucial role in enabling efficient and accurate scanning of target objects. However, recent reinforcement learning-based methods often require large-scale training data and still struggle to generalize to unseen object categories.In this work, we propose a data-efficient 3D scanning framework that uses Diffusion Policy to imitate human-like scanning strategies. To enhance robustness and generalization, we adopt the Occupancy Grid Mapping instead of direct point cloud processing, offering improved noise resilience and handling of diverse object geometries. We also introduce a hybrid approach combining a sphere-based space representation with a path optimization procedure that ensures path safety and scanning efficiency. This approach addresses limitations in conventional imitation learning, such as redundant or unpredictable behavior. We evaluate our method on diverse unseen objects in both shape and scale. Ours achieves higher coverage and shorter paths than baselines, while remaining robust to sensor noise. We further confirm practical feasibility and stable operation in real-world execution.

</details>


### [113] [Safe Probabilistic Planning for Human-Robot Interaction using Conformal Risk Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10392)
*Jake Gonzales,Kazuki Mizuta,Karen Leung,Lillian J. Ratliff*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出结合控制屏障函数与保形风险控制的新概率安全控制框架，为考虑复杂人类行为的人机交互提供形式化安全保证


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人机交互中需要确保安全，但人类行为复杂且难以预测。现有方法难以在考虑人类行为不确定性的同时提供形式化安全保证

Method: 结合控制屏障函数（CBFs）与保形风险控制（conformal risk control），量化CBF安全值的预测误差，根据交互上下文动态调整安全边界

Result: 在人机导航场景实验中，相比基线方法显著降低碰撞率和安全违规，同时保持高目标达成率和高效控制

Conclusion: 该框架能为人机交互提供概率安全保证，有效平衡安全性与任务性能，代码和仿真材料已公开

Abstract: In this paper, we present a novel probabilistic safe control framework for human-robot interaction that combines control barrier functions (CBFs) with conformal risk control to provide formal safety guarantees while considering complex human behavior. The approach uses conformal risk control to quantify and control the prediction errors in CBF safety values and establishes formal guarantees on the probability of constraint satisfaction during interaction. We introduce an algorithm that dynamically adjusts the safety margins produced by conformal risk control based on the current interaction context. Through experiments on human-robot navigation scenarios, we demonstrate that our approach significantly reduces collision rates and safety violations as compared to baseline methods while maintaining high success rates in goal-reaching tasks and efficient control. The code, simulations, and other supplementary material can be found on the project website: https://jakeagonzales.github.io/crc-cbf-website/.

</details>


### [114] [Shape Control of a Planar Hyper-Redundant Robot via Hybrid Kinematics-Informed and Learning-based Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.10402)
*Yuli Song,Wenbo Li,Wenci Xin,Zhiqiang Tang,Daniela Rus,Cecilia Laschi*
